</span></code></pre></div></div><p>The wizard asks whether you want to enable <strong>Tracing</strong> and <strong>Session Replay</strong>, route browser requests through your server to avoid ad blockers, and create an example page. To match the configuration in this guide, answer <strong>Yes</strong> to each.</p><p>Prefer to set things up yourself? Check out the <a class=" " data-sentry-element="Link" data-sentry-component="SmartLink" data-sentry-source-file="smartLink.tsx" href="/platforms/javascript/guides/nextjs/manual-setup/">Manual Setup</a> guide.</p><h2 id="configure"><a href="#configure" class="autolink-heading" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">Configure<svg class="anchorlink before" fill="currentColor" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M9.199 13.599a5.99 5.99 0 0 0 3.949 2.345 5.987 5.987 0 0 0 5.105-1.702l2.995-2.994a5.992 5.992 0 0 0 1.695-4.285 5.976 5.976 0 0 0-1.831-4.211 5.99 5.99 0 0 0-6.431-1.242 6.003 6.003 0 0 0-1.905 1.24l-1.731 1.721a.999.999 0 1 0 1.41 1.418l1.709-1.699a3.985 3.985 0 0 1 2.761-1.123 3.975 3.975 0 0 1 2.799 1.122 3.997 3.997 0 0 1 .111 5.644l-3.005 3.006a3.982 3.982 0 0 1-3.395 1.126 3.987 3.987 0 0 1-2.632-1.563A1 1 0 0 0 9.201 13.6zm5.602-3.198a5.99 5.99 0 0 0-3.949-2.345 5.987 5.987 0 0 0-5.105 1.702l-2.995 2.994a5.992 5.992 0 0 0-1.695 4.285 5.976 5.976 0 0 0 1.831 4.211 5.99 5.99 0 0 0 6.431 1.242 6.003 6.003 0 0 0 1.905-1.24l1.723-1.723a.999.999 0 1 0-1.414-1.414L9.836 19.81a3.985 3.985 0 0 1-2.761 1.123 3.975 3.975 0 0 1-2.799-1.122 3.997 3.997 0 0 1-.111-5.644l3.005-3.006a3.982 3.982 0 0 1 3.395-1.126 3.987 3.987 0 0 1 2.632 1.563 1 1 0 0 0 1.602-1.198z"></path></svg></a></h2><p><strong>Error Monitoring</strong> starts working as soon as you call <code>Sentry.init()</code>. <strong>Logs</strong> and <strong>Application Metrics</strong> need no extra configuration, and you&#x27;ll learn how to send them in steps <a href="#send-logs" class="">#3</a> and <a href="#send-application-metrics-" class="">#4</a>. This guide also enables <strong>Tracing</strong>, <strong>Session Replay</strong>, and <strong>User Feedback</strong>.</p><details class="style_expandable__WWChW callout !block callout-info" data-sentry-component="Expandable" data-sentry-source-file="index.tsx"><summary class="style_expandable-header__ICBo_ callout-header"><div class="style_expandable-title-container__b7sx3"><svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 15 15" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="callout-icon"><path d="M6.1584 3.13508C6.35985 2.94621 6.67627 2.95642 6.86514 3.15788L10.6151 7.15788C10.7954 7.3502 10.7954 7.64949 10.6151 7.84182L6.86514 11.8418C6.67627 12.0433 6.35985 12.0535 6.1584 11.8646C5.95694 11.6757 5.94673 11.3593 6.1356 11.1579L9.565 7.49985L6.1356 3.84182C5.94673 3.64036 5.95694 3.32394 6.1584 3.13508Z" fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><div>Want to learn more about these features?</div></div></summary><div class="style_expandable-body__lqvU6 callout-body content-flush-bottom"><div class="style_no-space-around-ul__jjZSQ" data-sentry-component="PlatformSection" data-sentry-source-file="index.tsx"><ul><li><a href="/product/issues"><strong>Issues</strong></a> (always enabled)<!-- -->:<!-- --> <!-- -->Sentry&#x27;s core error monitoring product that automatically reports errors, uncaught exceptions, and crashes. If you have something that looks like an exception, Sentry can capture it.</li><li><a href="/product/tracing"><strong>Tracing</strong></a>:<!-- --> <!-- -->Track software performance while seeing the impact of errors across multiple systems. For example, distributed tracing allows you to follow a request from the frontend to the backend and back.<!-- --> On the server, this includes<!-- --> <a href="/product/agents/">Agent Tracing</a>: supported AI libraries are instrumented automatically, so you can see model calls, tool executions, and token usage.</li><li><a href="/product/session-replay/"><strong>Session Replay</strong></a>:<!-- --> <!-- -->Get to the root cause of an issue faster by viewing a video-like reproduction of what users saw in your app before, during, and after the problem.</li><li><a href="/product/logs"><strong>Logs</strong></a>:<!-- --> <!-- -->Centralize and analyze your application logs to correlate them with errors and performance issues. Search, filter, and visualize log data to understand what&#x27;s happening in your applications.</li><li><a href="/product/metrics"><strong>Application Metrics</strong></a> (always enabled)<!-- -->:<!-- --> <!-- -->Track and analyze custom application metrics, such as response times and database query durations, to understand trends and patterns in your application&#x27;s performance and behavior over time.</li><li><a href="/product/user-feedback"><strong>User Feedback</strong></a>:<!-- --> <!-- -->Collect feedback directly from users when they encounter errors, allowing them to describe what happened and provide context that helps you understand and resolve issues faster.</li></ul></div></div></details><h3 id="initialize-the-sdk"><a href="#initialize-the-sdk" class="autolink-heading" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1">Initialize the SDK<svg class="anchorlink before" fill="currentColor" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M9.199 13.599a5.99 5.99 0 0 0 3.949 2.345 5.987 5.987 0 0 0 5.105-1.702l2.995-2.994a5.992 5.992 0 0 0 1.695-4.285 5.976 5.976 0 0 0-1.831-4.211 5.99 5.99 0 0 0-6.431-1.242 6.003 6.003 0 0 0-1.905 1.24l-1.731 1.721a.999.999 0 1 0 1.41 1.418l1.709-1.699a3.985 3.985 0 0 1 2.761-1.123 3.975 3.975 0 0 1 2.799 1.122 3.997 3.997 0 0 1 .111 5.644l-3.005 3.006a3.982 3.982 0 0 1-3.395 1.126 3.987 3.987 0 0 1-2.632-1.563A1 1 0 0 0 9.201 13.6zm5.602-3.198a5.99 5.99 0 0 0-3.949-2.345 5.987 5.987 0 0 0-5.105 1.702l-2.995 2.994a5.992 5.992 0 0 0-1.695 4.285 5.976 5.976 0 0 0 1.831 4.211 5.99 5.99 0 0 0 6.431 1.242 6.003 6.003 0 0 0 1.905-1.24l1.723-1.723a.999.999 0 1 0-1.414-1.414L9.836 19.81a3.985 3.985 0 0 1-2.761 1.123 3.975 3.975 0 0 1-2.799-1.122 the wizard creates a separate initialization file for each in your project&#x27;s root directory (or <code>src</code> folder):</p><ul><li><code>instrumentation-client.ts</code>: runs in the browser</li><li><code>sentry.server.config.ts</code>: runs in the Node.js runtime</li><li><code>sentry.edge.config.ts</code>: runs in the Edge runtime</li></ul><p>Use the tabs in the code box below to view each file.</p><p>The snippets also turn on:</p><ul><li>Tracing: via <a class=" " data-sentry-element="Link" data-sentry-component="SmartLink" data-sentry-source-file="smartLink.tsx" href="/platforms/javascript/guides/nextjs/configuration/options/#tracesSampleRate"><code>tracesSampleRate</code></a> in all three files</li><li>Session Replay: via <code>replayIntegration()</code>, sampled with <a class=" " data-sentry-element="Link" data-sentry-component="SmartLink" data-sentry-source-file="smartLink.tsx" href="/platforms/javascript/guides/nextjs/configuration/options/#replaysSessionSampleRate"><code>replaysSessionSampleRate</code></a> and <a class=" " data-sentry-element="Link" data-sentry-component="SmartLink" data-sentry-source-file="smartLink.tsx" href="/platforms/javascript/guides/nextjs/configuration/options/#replaysOnErrorSampleRate"><code>replaysOnErrorSampleRate</code></a></li><li>User Feedback: via <a class=" " data-sentry-element="Link" data-sentry-component="SmartLink" data-sentry-source-file="smartLink.tsx" href="/platforms/javascript/guides/nextjs/user-feedback/configuration/"><code>feedbackIntegration()</code></a>. The wizard doesn&#x27;t add this integration, so add it to <code>instrumentation-client.ts</code> yourself.</li></ul><p>Don&#x27;t need a feature? Remove its integration and options from the snippets.</p><div class="code-tabs-wrapper" data-code-tab-md-expand-tabs="true"><style data-emotion="css keyword">import</span> <span class="token operator">*</span> <span class="token keyword">as</span> Sentry <span class="token keyword">from</span> <span class="token string">&quot;@sentry/nextjs&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</span><span class="code-line">
</span><span class="code-line">Sentry<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">init</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  dsn<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;___PUBLIC_DSN___&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line" data-integrations-wrapper="open">  integrations<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    <span class="token comment">// enables Session Replay</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    Sentry<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">replayIntegration</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    <span class="token comment">// enables User Feedback</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    Sentry<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">feedbackIntegration</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span>
</span><span class="code-line">      <span class="token comment">// Additional integration configuration goes in here</span>
</span><span class="code-line">      colorScheme<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;system&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line" data-integrations-wrapper="close">  <span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// Enables Tracing.</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// Set to 1.0 to capture 100% of spans for tracing.</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// We recommend reducing this value in production.</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  tracesSampleRate<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// Capture replays for 10% of all sessions,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// plus for 100% of sessions with an error</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// Requires Sentry.replayIntegration()</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  replaysSessionSampleRate<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">0.1</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  replaysOnErrorSampleRate<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line"><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</span><span class="code-line">
</span><span class="code-line"><span class="token comment">// Instruments client-side navigations</span>
</span><span class="code-line"><span class="token keyword">export</span> <span class="token keyword">const</span> onRouterTransitionStart <span class="token operator">=</span> Sentry<span class="token punctuation">.</span>captureRouterTransitionStart<span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div data-code-tab-title="Client" hidden="" data-code-tab-filename="instrumentation-client.ts"><pre class="language-typescript"><code class="language-typescript code-highlight"><span class="code-line"><span class="token keyword">import</span> <span class="token operator">*</span> <span class="token keyword">as</span> Sentry <span class="token keyword">from</span> <span class="token string">&quot;@sentry/nextjs&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</span><span class="code-line">
</span><span class="code-line">Sentry<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">init</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  dsn<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;___PUBLIC_DSN___&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line" data-integrations-wrapper="open">  integrations<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    <span class="token comment">// enables Session Replay</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    Sentry<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">replayIntegration</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    <span class="token comment">// enables User Feedback</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    Sentry<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">feedbackIntegration</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span>
</span><span class="code-line">      <span class="token comment">// Additional integration configuration goes in here</span>
</span><span class="code-line">      colorScheme<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">&quot;system&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">    <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line" data-integrations-wrapper="close">  <span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// Enables Tracing.</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// Set to 1.0 to capture 100% of spans for tracing.</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// We recommend reducing this value in production.</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  tracesSampleRate<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// Capture replays for 10% of all sessions,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// plus for 100% of sessions with an error</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  <span class="token comment">// Requires Sentry.replayIntegration()</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  replaysSessionSampleRate<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">0.1</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line">  replaysOnErrorSampleRate<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
</span><span class="code-line"><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</span><span class="code-line">
</span><span class="code-line"><span class="token comment">// Instruments client-side navigations</span>
</span><span class="code-line"><span class="token keyword">export</span> <span class="token keyword">const</span> onRouterTransitionStart <span class="token operator">=</span> Sentry<span class="token punctuation">.</span>captureRouterTransitionStart<span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</span></code></pre></div><div data-code-tab-title="Server" hidden="" data-code-tab-filename="sentry.server.config.ts"><pre class="language-typescript"><code class="language-typescript code-highlight"><span class="code-line"><span class="token keyword">import</span> <span class="token operator">*</span> <span class="token keyword">as</span> Sentry <span class="token keyword">from</span> <span class="token string">&quot;@sentry/nextjs&quot;</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>

This instruments your app at build time, uploads source maps, and, if you said yes in the wizard, routes browser requests to Sentry through your server to avoid ad blockers.</p><p>To forward only the request body to Sentry, add a tunnel route handler (SDK version <code>10.77.1</code> and later on v10, <code>11.9.0</code> and later on v11). This will give you rich debugging context.</p><p>The SDK always filters sensitive values whose keys match a built-in denylist, such as <code>auth</code> or <code>password</code>, and sends <code>[Filtered]</code> instead.</p><p>To send less data, turn off the categories you don&#x27;t need in the <code>dataCollection</code> option.           --layout-total: calc(var(--sidebar-width, 300px) + var(--doc-content-w) + var(--toc-w));
          /* Fluid centering offset: 0 when viewport <= total layout width,
             grows equally on both sides when viewport > total layout width.
             This shifts the entire sidebar+content+ToC assembly toward center. */
          --layout-offset: max(0px, (100vw - var(--layout-total)) / 2);
        }
        #doc-content {
          max-width: var(--doc-content-w);
          box-sizing: border-box;
        }
        /* When the TOC is visible but the full layout doesn't fit yet, shrink content
           to fill available space rather than overflowing. Only applies when the TOC
           is actually rendered (has-toc class). */
        @media (min-width: 1490px) and (max-width: 1649px) {
          .has-toc #doc-content {
            max-width: calc(100vw - var(--sidebar-width, 300px) - var(--toc-w) - 2rem);
          }
        }
        /* Mobile responsive styles */
        @media (max-width: 768px) {
          .main-content {
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 100%;
            overflow-x: hidden;
          }
          #doc-content {
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 100%;
            overflow-x: hidden;
          }
        }
      </style></div><!--$--><!--/$--></div></div><script src="/_next/static/chunks/webpack-fbb425b2379b4e7d.js" id="_R_"
